### [39] [LucidNFT: LR-Anchored Multi-Reward Preference Optimization for Generative Real-World Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05947)
*Song Fei,Tian Ye,Sixiang Chen,Zhaohu Xing,Jianyu Lai,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LucidNFT提出了一种基于多奖励强化学习的流匹配真实世界图像超分辨率框架，通过引入LR锚定忠实度评估器、解耦优势归一化策略和大规模真实退化数据集，解决了现有方法中的语义结构幻觉、优势崩溃和退化覆盖不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式真实世界图像超分辨率存在关键问题：输出可能看起来清晰但不忠实于低分辨率输入证据（语义和结构幻觉），而LR锚定的忠实度难以评估。基于偏好的强化学习虽然适合，但受到三个主要限制：缺乏退化鲁棒的LR参考忠实度信号、rollout组优化瓶颈导致优势崩溃、以及真实退化覆盖有限。

Method: LucidNFT框架包含三个核心组件：1) LucidConsistency - 退化鲁棒的语义评估器，使LR锚定忠实度可测量和优化；2) 解耦优势归一化策略 - 在融合前保留每个LR条件rollout组内的目标间对比，防止优势崩溃；3) LucidLR - 大规模真实世界退化图像集合，支持鲁棒的RL微调。

Result: 实验表明，LucidNFT持续改进了基于流的真实世界超分辨率基线，在多样真实世界场景中实现了更好的感知-忠实度权衡，并具有稳定的优化动态。

Conclusion: LucidNFT通过解决生成式真实世界超分辨率中的关键挑战，包括LR锚定忠实度评估、多奖励优化瓶颈和退化多样性不足，为基于偏好的强化学习在图像超分辨率中的应用提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Generative real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) can synthesize visually convincing details from severely degraded low-resolution (LR) inputs, yet its stochastic sampling makes a critical failure mode hard to avoid: outputs may look sharp but be unfaithful to the LR evidence (semantic and structural hallucination), while such LR-anchored faithfulness is difficult to assess without HR ground truth. Preference-based reinforcement learning (RL) is a natural fit because each LR input yields a rollout group of candidates to compare. However, effective alignment in Real-ISR is hindered by (i) the lack of a degradation-robust LR-referenced faithfulness signal, and (ii) a rollout-group optimization bottleneck where naive multi-reward scalarization followed by normalization compresses objective-wise contrasts, causing advantage collapse and weakening the reward-weighted updates in DiffusionNFT-style forward fine-tuning. Moreover, (iii) limited coverage of real degradations restricts rollout diversity and preference signal quality. We propose LucidNFT, a multi-reward RL framework for flow-matching Real-ISR. LucidNFT introduces LucidConsistency, a degradation-robust semantic evaluator that makes LR-anchored faithfulness measurable and optimizable; a decoupled advantage normalization strategy that preserves objective-wise contrasts within each LR-conditioned rollout group before fusion, preventing advantage collapse; and LucidLR, a large-scale collection of real-world degraded images to support robust RL fine-tuning. Experiments show that LucidNFT consistently improves strong flow-based Real-ISR baselines, achieving better perceptual-faithfulness trade-offs with stable optimization dynamics across diverse real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [40] [Imagine How To Change: Explicit Procedure Modeling for Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05969)
*Jiayang Sun,Zixin Guo,Min Cao,Guibo Zhu,Jorma Laaksonen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProCap将变化描述任务从静态图像对比重构为动态过程建模，通过两阶段框架学习变化过程的潜在动态表示，在三个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变化描述方法基于静态图像对，忽略了变化过程的丰富时间动态信息，而这些动态信息对于理解"发生了什么变化"以及"如何发生"至关重要。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段训练过程编码器，通过自动生成中间帧获取关键帧，通过caption-conditioned掩码重建任务学习潜在动态；第二阶段将训练好的编码器集成到编码器-解码器模型中，引入可学习的过程查询来推断潜在过程表示，然后解码为文本描述。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验证明了ProCap的有效性，代码和预训练模型已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将变化建模从静态图像比较重构为动态过程建模，ProCap能够更好地理解变化过程的时间动态，生成更准确的变化描述。

Abstract: Change captioning generates descriptions that explicitly describe the differences between two visually similar images. Existing methods operate on static image pairs, thus ignoring the rich temporal dynamics of the change procedure, which is the key to understand not only what has changed but also how it occurs. We introduce ProCap, a novel framework that reformulates change modeling from static image comparison to dynamic procedure modeling. ProCap features a two-stage design: The first stage trains a procedure encoder to learn the change procedure from a sparse set of keyframes. These keyframes are obtained by automatically generating intermediate frames to make the implicit procedural dynamics explicit and then sampling them to mitigate redundancy. Then the encoder learns to capture the latent dynamics of these keyframes via a caption-conditioned, masked reconstruction task. The second stage integrates this trained encoder within an encoder-decoder model for captioning. Instead of relying on explicit frames from the previous stage -- a process incurring computational overhead and sensitivity to visual noise -- we introduce learnable procedure queries to prompt the encoder for inferring the latent procedure representation, which the decoder then translates into text. The entire model is then trained end-to-end with a captioning loss, ensuring the encoder's output is both temporally coherent and captioning-aligned. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ProCap. Code and pre-trained models are available at https://github.com/BlueberryOreo/ProCap

</details>


### [41] [DeepSight: Bridging Depth Maps and Language with a Depth-Driven Multimodal Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06090)
*Hao Yang,Hongbo Zhang,Yanyan Zhao,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeepSight是首个专门用于深度感知的多模态大语言模型，通过构建深度图像-文本对数据集和改进视觉编码器，显著提升了三维场景理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在图像描述和视觉问答等任务上表现优异，但往往难以准确理解视觉数据中的深度信息。深度信息对于三维场景理解至关重要，而传统方法主要关注RGB图像与文本的对齐，忽略了深度图像的特殊性。

Method: 1. 构建新颖的深度图像-文本对数据集和深度指令数据集：使用GLPN模型从视觉图像生成深度图，利用GPT-4生成对应的深度指令，并通过LLaVA验证。2. 改进CLIP中的ViT编码器：融入局部物体信息，以更好地捕捉深度图的细微连续变化。3. 开发全面的深度问答基准：基于现有深度图像数据集评估模型在典型深度图场景中的理解能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，DeepSight显著提升了深度感知能力和下游任务性能，在多模态三维理解方面取得了实质性进展。

Conclusion: DeepSight作为首个专门的深度多模态大语言模型，通过利用深度图像的特性和改进的视觉编码器，有效解决了现有模型在深度信息理解方面的不足，为三维场景理解提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance across various tasks such as image captioning and visual question answer(VQA); however, they often struggle to accurately interpret depth information inherent in visual data. In this work, we introduce DeepSight, the first dedicated depth MLLM designed to enhance three-dimensional scene understanding. Unlike conventional methods that align RGB image encodings with text, our approach takes advantage of the unique characteristics of depth images: single-channel grayscale images where the pixel values directly reflect depth cues to improve spatial reasoning. To address challenges associated with limited depth data and the inadequacy of simple channel replication, we construct a novel depth image-text pair dataset and a depth instruction dataset. Depth maps are generated from visual images using the GLPN model, and GPT-4 is employed to curate corresponding depth instructions, an approach validated by LLaVA. Additionally, we modify the ViT encoder in CLIP to incorporate local object information, thereby capturing the subtle continuous variations of depth more effectively. To evaluate the performance of our model, we develop a comprehensive depth question answer benchmark based on existing depth image datasets, which rigorously assesses understanding in typical depth map scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that DeepSight significantly enhances depth perception and downstream task performance, marking a substantial step forward in multimodal three-dimensional understanding.

</details>


### [42] [Unify the Views: View-Consistent Prototype Learning for Few-Shot Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05952)
*Hongli Liu,Yu Wang,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VINE提出了一种统一框架，通过联合建模结构一致性和前景判别性来改进少样本分割中的类别特定原型，特别针对视角变化和结构错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本分割在有限监督下泛化到新类别时面临结构错位和跨视角不一致的挑战，特别是在外观或视角变化大的情况下。现有方法在处理这些挑战时效果有限。

Method: VINE框架包含：1）在骨干特征上构建空间-视角图，空间图捕捉局部几何拓扑，视角图连接不同视角特征传播视角不变的结构语义；2）从支持-查询特征差异中提取判别性先验，捕获类别特定对比度；3）通过掩码交叉注意力逐步集成前景增强的SAM特征和结构丰富的ResNet特征，生成类别一致的原型作为SAM解码器的自适应提示。

Result: 在多个少样本分割基准测试上的广泛实验验证了VINE的有效性和鲁棒性，特别是在具有视角偏移和复杂结构的挑战性场景下表现优异。

Conclusion: VINE通过联合建模结构一致性和前景判别性，成功解决了少样本分割中的结构错位和跨视角不一致问题，为处理视角变化和复杂结构提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Few-shot segmentation (FSS) has gained significant attention for its ability to generalize to novel classes with limited supervision, yet remains challenged by structural misalignment and cross-view inconsistency under large appearance or viewpoint variations. This paper tackles these challenges by introducing VINE (View-Informed NEtwork), a unified framework that jointly models structural consistency and foreground discrimination to refine class-specific prototypes. Specifically, VINE introduces a spatial-view graph on backbone features, where the spatial graph captures local geometric topology and the view graph connects features from different perspectives to propagate view-invariant structural semantics. To further alleviate foreground ambiguity, we derive a discriminative prior from the support-query feature discrepancy to capture category-specific contrast, which reweights SAM features by emphasizing salient regions and recalibrates backbone activations for improved structural focus. The foreground-enhanced SAM features and structurally enriched ResNet features are progressively integrated through masked cross-attention, yielding class-consistent prototypes used as adaptive prompts for the SAM decoder to generate accurate masks. Extensive experiments on multiple FSS benchmarks validate the effectiveness and robustness of VINE, particularly under challenging scenarios with viewpoint shifts and complex structures. The code is available at https://github.com/HongliLiu1/VINE-main.

</details>


### [43] [Contrastive-to-Self-Supervised: A Two-Stage Framework for Script Similarity Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06180)
*Claire Roman,Philippe Meyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架解决字形和书写系统相似性度量问题：第一阶段在有标签的人工字母表上训练对比学习编码器作为教师模型；第二阶段通过师生蒸馏扩展到历史书写系统，学生模型在教师指导下学习无监督表示，发现跨文字的潜在相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字形和书写系统相似性度量面临根本挑战：人工字母表中的单个字素可以可靠标注，但不同文字之间的历史关系存在不确定性和争议。需要一种方法既能处理有标签数据，又能扩展到历史关系不确定的书写系统。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）在有标签的人工字母表上使用对比损失训练编码器，建立具有鲁棒判别特征的教师模型；2）通过师生蒸馏扩展到历史文字，学生模型在教师指导下学习无监督表示，但可以自由发现跨文字的潜在相似性。非对称设置使学生能够学习变形不变嵌入，同时继承干净示例的判别结构。

Result: 在多种书写系统上的实验表明，该方法实现了有效的少样本字形识别和有意义的文字聚类，无需真实进化关系作为监督信号。

Conclusion: 该方法桥接了监督对比学习和无监督发现，既能建立不同系统之间的硬边界，又能反映潜在历史影响的软相似性，为解决字形和书写系统相似性度量问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Learning similarity metrics for glyphs and writing systems faces a fundamental challenge: while individual graphemes within invented alphabets can be reliably labeled, the historical relationships between different scripts remain uncertain and contested. We propose a two-stage framework that addresses this epistemological constraint. First, we train an encoder with contrastive loss on labeled invented alphabets, establishing a teacher model with robust discriminative features. Second, we extend to historically attested scripts through teacher-student distillation, where the student learns unsupervised representations guided by the teacher's knowledge but free to discover latent cross-script similarities. The asymmetric setup enables the student to learn deformation-invariant embeddings while inheriting discriminative structure from clean examples. Our approach bridges supervised contrastive learning and unsupervised discovery, enabling both hard boundaries between distinct systems and soft similarities reflecting potential historical influences. Experiments on diverse writing systems demonstrate effective few-shot glyph recognition and meaningful script clustering without requiring ground-truth evolutionary relationships.

</details>


### [44] [OVGGT: O(1) Constant-Cost Streaming Visual Geometry Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05959)
*Si-Yu Lu,Po-Ting Chen,Hui-Che Hsu,Sin-Ye Jhong,Wen-Huang Cheng,Yung-Yao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVGGT：一种训练免费框架，用于从流视频中重建3D几何，通过自选择缓存和动态锚点保护实现恒定内存和计算开销，支持任意长视频处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何基础模型使用全注意力机制，计算成本呈二次增长，只能处理短序列；因果注意力变体如StreamVGGT虽然支持单次流式处理，但KV缓存会不断增长，在数百帧内耗尽GPU内存，无法实现真正意义上的长序列流式推理。

Method: 提出OVGGT框架：1）自选择缓存：利用FFN残差幅度压缩KV缓存，与FlashAttention完全兼容；2）动态锚点保护：保护坐标关键令牌不被驱逐，抑制长轨迹中的几何漂移。该框架无需训练，无论序列长度如何，都能将内存和计算限制在固定预算内。

Result: 在室内、室外和超长序列基准测试上的广泛实验表明，OVGGT能够在恒定VRAM限制下处理任意长视频，同时实现最先进的3D几何精度。

Conclusion: OVGGT解决了流式视频3D重建中的内存和计算瓶颈，通过创新的缓存压缩和关键令牌保护机制，实现了真正意义上的长序列流式推理，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D geometry from streaming video requires continuous inference under bounded resources. Recent geometric foundation models achieve impressive reconstruction quality through all-to-all attention, yet their quadratic cost confines them to short, offline sequences. Causal-attention variants such as StreamVGGT enable single-pass streaming but accumulate an ever-growing KV cache, exhausting GPU memory within hundreds of frames and precluding the long-horizon deployment that motivates streaming inference in the first place. We present OVGGT, a training-free framework that bounds both memory and compute to a fixed budget regardless of sequence length. Our approach combines Self-Selective Caching, which leverages FFN residual magnitudes to compress the KV cache while remaining fully compatible with FlashAttention, with Dynamic Anchor Protection, which shields coordinate-critical tokens from eviction to suppress geometric drift over extended trajectories. Extensive experiments on indoor, outdoor, and ultra-long sequence benchmarks demonstrate that OVGGT processes arbitrarily long videos within a constant VRAM envelope while achieving state-of-the-art 3D geometric accuracy.

</details>


### [45] [Exploring Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition in Images using CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05962)
*Wei Yu Chen,Ying Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于两阶段策略（目标分割+识别）的开放词汇目标识别框架，无需复杂重训练和人工标注，通过CLIP生成图像和文本嵌入，或使用CNN/MLP方法提取特征，利用SVD构建共享表示空间，通过嵌入相似度匹配进行识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇目标识别方法存在系统复杂度高、训练成本大、泛化能力有限的问题，需要一种更简洁高效的解决方案。

Method: 采用两阶段策略：1）目标分割；2）识别。裁剪目标区域后，使用CLIP生成目标级图像嵌入和类别级文本嵌入，或引入CNN/MLP方法提取卷积特征并用MLP对齐视觉特征与文本嵌入。通过SVD构建共享表示空间，最后通过嵌入相似度匹配进行识别。

Result: 在COCO、Pascal VOC和ADE20K数据集上的实验表明，无需训练的CLIP编码（无SVD）获得了最高的平均AP，超越了当前最先进方法。同时，CNN/MLP图像编码方法在开放词汇目标识别中显示出潜力。

Conclusion: 提出的开放词汇目标识别框架有效解决了现有方法的局限性，无需复杂训练和标注，CLIP编码方法表现最佳，CNN/MLP方法也展现出应用前景，为开放词汇识别提供了简洁高效的解决方案。

Abstract: To address the limitations of existing open-vocabulary object recognition methods, specifically high system complexity, substantial training costs, and limited generalization, this paper proposes a novel Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition (OVOR) framework based on a streamlined two-stage strategy: object segmentation followed by recognition. The framework eliminates the need for complex retraining and labor-intensive annotation. After cropping object regions, we generate object-level image embeddings alongside category-level text embeddings using CLIP, which facilitates arbitrary vocabularies. To reduce reliance on CLIP and enhance encoding flexibility, we further introduce a CNN/MLP-based method that extracts convolutional neural network (CNN) feature maps and utilizes a multilayer perceptron (MLP) to align visual features with text embeddings. These embeddings are concatenated and processed via Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) to construct a shared representation space. Finally, recognition is performed through embedding similarity matching. Experiments on COCO, Pascal VOC, and ADE20K demonstrate that training-free, CLIP-based encoding without SVD achieves the highest average AP, outperforming current state-of-the-art methods. Simultaneously, the results highlight the potential of CNN/MLP-based image encoding for OVOR.

</details>


### [46] [Skeleton-to-Image Encoding: Enabling Skeleton Representation Learning via Vision-Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05963)
*Siyuan Yang,Jun Liu,Hao Cheng,Chong Wang,Shijian Lu,Hedvig Kjellstrom,Weisi Lin,Alex C. Kot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S2I方法将骨架序列转换为图像格式，使预训练视觉模型能用于自监督骨架表示学习，解决了骨架数据格式差异和大规模数据集稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模预训练视觉模型难以直接应用于3D人体骨架数据，因为数据格式存在根本差异。同时，大规模骨架数据集稀缺，且需要在不增加额外模型分支的情况下将骨架数据融入多模态动作识别。

Method: 提出Skeleton-to-Image Encoding (S2I)表示方法，将骨架序列转换为类图像数据：基于身体部位语义对关节进行分区和排列，并调整到标准图像尺寸。

Result: 在NTU-60、NTU-120和PKU-MMD数据集上的实验证明了该方法在自监督骨架表示学习中的有效性和泛化能力，包括在具有挑战性的跨格式评估设置下。

Conclusion: S2I表示首次实现了使用强大的视觉预训练模型进行自监督骨架表示学习，将丰富的视觉领域知识有效转移到骨架分析中，并为异构骨架数据提供了统一的图像格式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large-scale pretrained vision models have demonstrated impressive capabilities across a wide range of downstream tasks, including cross-modal and multi-modal scenarios. However, their direct application to 3D human skeleton data remains challenging due to fundamental differences in data format. Moreover, the scarcity of large-scale skeleton datasets and the need to incorporate skeleton data into multi-modal action recognition without introducing additional model branches present significant research opportunities. To address these challenges, we introduce Skeleton-to-Image Encoding (S2I), a novel representation that transforms skeleton sequences into image-like data by partitioning and arranging joints based on body-part semantics and resizing to standardized image dimensions. This encoding enables, for the first time, the use of powerful vision-pretrained models for self-supervised skeleton representation learning, effectively transferring rich visual-domain knowledge to skeleton analysis. While existing skeleton methods often design models tailored to specific, homogeneous skeleton formats, they overlook the structural heterogeneity that naturally arises from diverse data sources. In contrast, our S2I representation offers a unified image-like format that naturally accommodates heterogeneous skeleton data. Extensive experiments on NTU-60, NTU-120, and PKU-MMD demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of our method for self-supervised skeleton representation learning, including under challenging cross-format evaluation settings.

</details>


### [47] [CR-QAT: Curriculum Relational Quantization-Aware Training for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05964)
*Jinyeong Park,Donghwa Kim,Brent ByungHoon Kang,Hyeongboo Baek,Jibum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CR-QAT框架通过课程量化感知训练和文本中心关系知识蒸馏，在极端低比特（如4位）量化下显著提升开放词汇目标检测的性能，解决量化导致的视觉-语言对齐退化和关系结构扭曲问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇目标检测（OVOD）通过视觉-语言对齐实现新类别检测，但大模型尺寸限制了在资源受限设备上的部署。量化提供实用压缩方案，但极端低比特量化会严重降低细粒度视觉-语言对齐质量并扭曲区域间关系结构。

Method: 提出课程关系量化感知训练（CR-QAT）框架，包含两个核心组件：1）课程QAT（CQAT）：通过分阶段模型分区进行渐进量化，实现误差隔离和稳定优化；2）文本中心关系知识蒸馏（TRKD）：针对任务相关模块，通过构建文本锚定的成对相似度矩阵，全面传递教师模型的多维关系知识。

Result: 在LVIS和COCO零样本基准测试中，CR-QAT在极端低比特设置下持续优于现有QAT基线方法，分别实现了相对AP提升高达38.9%和40.9%。

Conclusion: CR-QAT框架有效解决了开放词汇目标检测中极端低比特量化导致的视觉-语言对齐退化和关系结构扭曲问题，为资源受限设备上的高效部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVOD) enables novel category detection via vision-language alignment, but massive model sizes hinder deployment on resource-constrained devices. While quantization offers practical compression, we reveal that naive extreme low-bit (e.g., 4-bit) quantization severely degrades fine-grained vision-language alignment and distorts inter-region relational structures. To address this, we propose curriculum relational quantization-aware training (CR-QAT), an integrated framework combining stage-by-stage optimization with relational knowledge distillation. Within CR-QAT, curriculum QAT (CQAT) mitigates error accumulation by partitioning the model for progressive quantization, ensuring stable optimization via error isolation. Concurrently, text-centric relational KD (TRKD) is applied to task-relevant modules. By constructing text-anchored pairwise similarity matrices, TRKD comprehensively transfers the teacher's multi-dimensional relational knowledge. Experiments on LVIS and COCO zero-shot benchmarks demonstrate that CR-QAT consistently outperforms existing QAT baselines under aggressive low-bit settings, achieving relative AP improvements of up to 38.9% and 40.9%, respectively.

</details>


### [48] [Breaking Smooth-Motion Assumptions: A UAV Benchmark for Multi-Object Tracking in Complex and Adverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05970)
*Jingtao Ye,Kexin Zhang,Xunchi Ma,Yuehan Li,Guangming Zhu,Peiyi Shen,Linhua Jiang,Xiangdong Zhang,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynUAV是一个新的无人机视角多目标跟踪基准，专注于剧烈自运动和复杂表观轨迹，包含42个视频序列和170万+标注框，相比现有基准更具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视角多目标跟踪基准缺乏无人机快速移动和敏捷机动带来的观测挑战，通常只包含可预测的相机动态和线性运动模式，无法反映真实世界的复杂性。

Method: 创建DynUAV基准，包含42个视频序列，超过170万个边界框标注，涵盖车辆、行人以及挖掘机、推土机、起重机等工业类别，特别强调剧烈自运动带来的挑战。

Result: 对最先进的跟踪器在DynUAV上的综合评估显示，它们在处理动态条件下的检测和关联交织挑战方面存在明显局限性，特别是在剧烈尺度变化、视角变化和运动模糊方面。

Conclusion: DynUAV作为一个严格的基准，将推动真实世界无人机视角多目标跟踪的进展，所有资源将公开提供。

Abstract: The rapid movements and agile maneuvers of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) induce significant observational challenges for multi-object tracking (MOT). However, existing UAV-perspective MOT benchmarks often lack these complexities, featuring predominantly predictable camera dynamics and linear motion patterns. To address this gap, we introduce DynUAV, a new benchmark for dynamic UAV-perspective MOT, characterized by intense ego-motion and the resulting complex apparent trajectories. The benchmark comprises 42 video sequences with over 1.7 million bounding box annotations, covering vehicles, pedestrians, and specialized industrial categories such as excavators, bulldozers and cranes. Compared to existing benchmarks, DynUAV introduces substantial challenges arising from ego-motion, including drastic scale changes and viewpoint changes, as well as motion blur. Comprehensive evaluations of state-of-the-art trackers on DynUAV reveal their limitations, particularly in managing the intertwined challenges of detection and association under such dynamic conditions, thereby establishing DynUAV as a rigorous benchmark. We anticipate that DynUAV will serve as a demanding testbed to spur progress in real-world UAV-perspective MOT, and we will make all resources available at link.

</details>


### [49] [Technical Report: Automated Optical Inspection of Surgical Instruments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05987)
*Zunaira Shafqat,Atif Aftab Ahmed Jilani,Qurrat Ul Ain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告研究了巴基斯坦制造的手术器械及其制造缺陷，通过与行业领先企业合作，开发了包含4,414张高分辨率图像的数据集，并利用YOLOv8、ResNet-152和EfficientNet-b4等深度学习架构实现自动缺陷检测，以提高手术器械质量标准和患者安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代医疗环境中，手术器械的质量标准对临床成功至关重要。即使是微小的制造缺陷也可能导致致命后果，因此需要精确的制造工艺。报告旨在解决手术器械中裂纹、锈蚀和结构不规则等关键缺陷的识别与纠正问题，防止制造商重大经济损失，更重要的是保护患者生命安全。

Method: 通过与Sialkot手术器械集群的行业领导者Daddy D Pro和Dr. Frigz International合作，开发了包含4,414张高分辨率图像的新数据集。采用深度学习架构包括YOLOv8、ResNet-152和EfficientNet-b4进行自动缺陷检测，专注于通过自动光学检测工具实现质量保证。

Result: 报告提供了对巴基斯坦制造的各种手术器械及其相关缺陷的分析，建立了专门的数据集用于缺陷检测研究。通过与行业专家的合作，获得了对巴基斯坦制造器械缺陷的宝贵见解，为制造商、医疗专业人员和监管机构提供了资源。

Conclusion: 该研究通过结合行业专业知识和尖端技术，特别是深度学习架构的应用，提升了手术器械制造过程中的质量标准。这些见解有助于增强器械标准，确保更可靠的医疗环境，展示了自动缺陷检测方法在提高手术器械质量和患者安全方面的重要价值。

Abstract: In the dynamic landscape of modern healthcare, maintaining the highest standards in surgical instruments is critical for clinical success. This report explores the diverse realm of surgical instruments and their associated manufacturing defects, emphasizing their pivotal role in ensuring the safety of surgical procedures. With potentially fatal consequences arising from even minor defects, precision in manufacturing is paramount.The report addresses the identification and rectification of critical defects such as cracks, rust, and structural irregularities. Such scrutiny prevents substantial financial losses for manufacturers and, more crucially, safeguards patient lives. The collaboration with industry leaders Daddy D Pro and Dr. Frigz International, renowned trailblazers in the Sialkot surgical cluster, provides invaluable insights into the analysis of defects in Pakistani-made instruments. This partnership signifies a commitment to advancing automated defect detection methodologies, specifically through the integration of deep learning architectures including YOLOv8, ResNet-152, and EfficientNet-b4, thereby elevating quality standards in the manufacturing process. The scope of this report is to identify various surgical instruments manufactured in Pakistan and analyze their associated defects using a newly developed dataset of 4,414 high-resolution images. By focusing on quality assurance through Automated Optical Inspection (AOI) tools, this document serves as a resource for manufacturers, healthcare professionals, and regulatory bodies. The insights gained contribute to the enhancement of instrument standards, ensuring a more reliable healthcare environment through industry expertise and cutting-edge technology.

</details>


### [50] [MM-ISTS: Cooperating Irregularly Sampled Time Series Forecasting with Multimodal Vision-Text LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05997)
*Zhi Lei,Chenxi Liu,Hao Miao,Wanghui Qiu,Bin Yang,Chenjuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MM-ISTS是一个多模态框架，利用视觉-文本大语言模型增强不规则采样时间序列预测，通过跨模态编码和自适应特征提取提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不规则采样时间序列预测方法仅使用历史观测值，难以学习上下文语义和细粒度时间模式，需要更全面的多模态理解。

Method: 提出两阶段编码机制：1)跨模态视觉-文本编码模块自动生成视觉图像和文本数据，结合MLLMs捕获复杂时间模式和上下文理解；2)ISTS编码提取历史观测的丰富时间特征；3)自适应查询特征提取器压缩MLLMs学习到的token；4)多模态对齐模块缓解不同模态间的差距。

Result: 在真实数据上的广泛实验证明了所提解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: MM-ISTS通过整合时间、视觉和文本模态，有效提升了不规则采样时间序列的预测能力，为多模态时间序列分析提供了新思路。

Abstract: Irregularly sampled time series (ISTS) are widespread in real-world scenarios, exhibiting asynchronous observations on uneven time intervals across variables. Existing ISTS forecasting methods often solely utilize historical observations to predict future ones while falling short in learning contextual semantics and fine-grained temporal patterns. To address these problems, we achieve MM-ISTS, a multimodal framework augmented by vision-text large language models, that bridges temporal, visual, and textual modalities, facilitating ISTS forecasting. MM-ISTS encompasses a novel two-stage encoding mechanism. In particular, a cross-modal vision-text encoding module is proposed to automatically generate informative visual images and textual data, enabling the capture of intricate temporal patterns and comprehensive contextual understanding, in collaboration with multimodal LLMs (MLLMs). In parallel, ISTS encoding extracts complementary yet enriched temporal features from historical ISTS observations, including multi-view embedding fusion and a temporal-variable encoder. Further, we propose an adaptive query-based feature extractor to compress the learned tokens of MLLMs, filtering out small-scale useful knowledge, which in turn reduces computational costs. In addition, a multimodal alignment module with modality-aware gating is designed to alleviate the modality gap across ISTS, images, and text. Extensive experiments on real data offer insight into the effectiveness of the proposed solutions.

</details>


### [51] [RePer-360: Releasing Perspective Priors for 360$^\circ$ Depth Estimation via Self-Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05999)
*Cheng Guan,Chunyu Lin,Zhijie Shen,Junsong Zhang,Jiyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RePer-360是一个用于单目全景深度估计的失真感知自调制框架，通过轻量级几何对齐引导模块和自条件AdaLN-Zero机制，在保持预训练透视先验的同时适应全景域，仅需1%训练数据即可超越标准微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度基础模型在透视图像上训练效果良好，但在360°全景图像上泛化能力差，因为透视域和全景域之间存在显著的几何差异。完全微调这些模型通常需要大量全景数据，这限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出RePer-360框架：1）设计轻量级几何对齐引导模块，从ERP和CP两种互补投影中提取调制信号，引导模型适应全景域而不覆盖预训练透视知识；2）引入自条件AdaLN-Zero机制，生成像素级缩放因子以减少透视域和全景域之间的特征分布差距；3）使用立方体贴图域一致性损失提高训练稳定性和跨投影对齐。

Result: RePer-360仅使用1%的训练数据就超越了标准微调方法。在相同域内训练设置下，RMSE指标实现了约20%的改进。该方法在保持预训练透视先验的同时有效适应全景域。

Conclusion: 通过将重点从互补投影融合转向在保持预训练透视先验下的全景域适应，RePer-360提供了一种高效的单目全景深度估计解决方案，显著减少了数据需求并提升了性能。

Abstract: Recent depth foundation models trained on perspective imagery achieve strong performance, yet generalize poorly to 360$^\circ$ images due to the substantial geometric discrepancy between perspective and panoramic domains. Moreover, fully fine-tuning these models typically requires large amounts of panoramic data. To address this issue, we propose RePer-360, a distortion-aware self-modulation framework for monocular panoramic depth estimation that adapts depth foundation models while preserving powerful pretrained perspective priors. Specifically, we design a lightweight geometry-aligned guidance module to derive a modulation signal from two complementary projections (i.e., ERP and CP) and use it to guide the model toward the panoramic domain without overwriting its pretrained perspective knowledge. We further introduce a Self-Conditioned AdaLN-Zero mechanism that produces pixel-wise scaling factors to reduce the feature distribution gap between the perspective and panoramic domains. In addition, a cubemap-domain consistency loss further improves training stability and cross-projection alignment. By shifting the focus from complementary-projection fusion to panoramic domain adaptation under preserved pretrained perspective priors, RePer-360 surpasses standard fine-tuning methods while using only 1\% of the training data. Under the same in-domain training setting, it further achieves an approximately 20\% improvement in RMSE. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [52] [Demystifying KAN for Vision Tasks: The RepKAN Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06002)
*Minjong Cheon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RepKAN是一种新型遥感图像分类架构，结合CNN的结构效率和KAN的非线性表示能力，通过双路径设计实现可解释的物理推理，在多个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前遥感图像分类中，标准CNN和Transformer通常作为不可解释的黑盒模型运行，缺乏对分类决策的物理可解释性。需要一种既能保持高性能又能提供明确物理解释的架构。

Method: 提出RepKAN架构，集成CNN的结构效率和KAN（Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks）的非线性表示能力。采用双路径设计：空间线性路径和光谱非线性路径，能够自主发现类别特定的光谱指纹和物理交互流形。

Result: 在EuroSAT和NWPU-RESISC45数据集上的实验结果表明，RepKAN在提供明确的物理可解释推理的同时，性能超越了最先进的模型。

Conclusion: RepKAN具有显著潜力作为未来可解释视觉基础模型的主干架构，为遥感图像分类提供了性能与可解释性兼备的解决方案。

Abstract: Remote sensing image classification is essential for Earth observation, yet standard CNNs and Transformers often function as uninterpretable black-boxes. We propose RepKAN, a novel architecture that integrates the structural efficiency of CNNs with the non-linear representational power of KANs. By utilizing a dual-path design -- Spatial Linear and Spectral Non-linear -- RepKAN enables the autonomous discovery of class-specific spectral fingerprints and physical interaction manifolds. Experimental results on the EuroSAT and NWPU-RESISC45 datasets demonstrate that RepKAN provides explicit physically interpretable reasoning while outperforming state-of-the-art models. These findings indicate that RepKAN holds significant potential to serve as the backbone for future interpretable visual foundation models.

</details>


### [53] [EffectMaker: Unifying Reasoning and Generation for Customized Visual Effect Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06014)
*Shiyuan Yang,Ruihuang Li,Jiale Tao,Shuai Shao,Qinglin Lu,Jing Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EffectMaker是一个统一的推理-生成框架，通过多模态大语言模型理解高级特效语义，结合扩散变换器进行上下文学习，实现无需逐特效微调的参考视频特效定制化生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高质量视觉特效制作需要专业知识和高成本流程，现有AIGC系统面临特效特定数据稀缺、超自然/风格化效果建模困难等挑战，且通常需要逐特效微调，限制了其可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: 采用多模态大语言模型解释高级特效语义并推理如何适配目标主体，同时使用扩散变换器通过上下文学习从参考视频中捕捉细粒度视觉线索，形成语义-视觉双路径引导机制。构建了包含130k视频、3k特效类别的EffectData数据集。

Result: 实验表明EffectMaker在视觉质量和特效一致性方面优于现有最先进基线方法，提供了可扩展且灵活的自定义特效生成范式。

Conclusion: EffectMaker通过统一的推理-生成框架解决了现有AIGC系统在视觉特效生成中的关键挑战，实现了无需逐特效微调的准确、可控、特效一致的合成，为定制化VFX生成提供了可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Visual effects (VFX) are essential for enhancing the expressiveness and creativity of video content, yet producing high-quality effects typically requires expert knowledge and costly production pipelines. Existing AIGC systems face significant challenges in VFX generation due to the scarcity of effect-specific data and the inherent difficulty of modeling supernatural or stylized effects. Moreover, these approaches often require per-effect fine-tuning, which severely limits their scalability and generalization to novel VFX. In this work, we present EffectMaker, a unified reasoning-generation framework that enables reference-based VFX customization. EffectMaker employs a multimodal large language model to interpret high-level effect semantics and reason about how they should adapt to a target subject, while a diffusion transformer leverages in-context learning to capture fine-grained visual cues from reference videos. These two components form a semantic-visual dual-path guidance mechanism that enables accurate, controllable, and effect-consistent synthesis without per-effect fine-tuning. Furthermore, we construct EffectData, the largest high-quality synthetic dataset containing 130k videos across 3k VFX categories, to improve generalization and scalability. Experiments show that EffectMaker achieves superior visual quality and effect consistency over state-of-the-art baselines, offering a scalable and flexible paradigm for customized VFX generation. Project page: https://effectmaker.github.io

</details>


### [54] [MOSIV: Multi-Object System Identification from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06022)
*Chunjiang Liu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Qingran Lin,Albert Xiao,Haoyu Chen,Shizheng Wen,Hao Zhang,Lu Qi,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Laszlo A. Jeni,Min Xu,Yizhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOSIV框架通过可微分模拟器直接从视频中优化连续、逐对象的材料参数，解决多物体系统识别问题，在接触丰富的多物体交互场景中显著提升定位精度和长期模拟保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要针对单物体场景或使用固定材料原型的离散材料分类，无法有效处理多物体系统识别问题，特别是在接触丰富的多物体交互场景中。

Method: 提出MOSIV框架，使用可微分模拟器直接从视频中优化连续、逐对象的材料参数，通过几何对齐的目标函数指导优化过程，并创建了包含接触丰富多物体交互的合成基准数据集。

Result: 在新建的基准数据集上，MOSIV相比适应后的基线方法显著提高了定位精度和长期模拟保真度，成为该新任务的强基线方法。

Conclusion: 物体级别的细粒度监督和几何对齐的目标函数对于复杂多物体设置中的稳定优化至关重要，MOSIV为多物体系统识别任务建立了新的基准。

Abstract: We introduce the challenging problem of multi-object system identification from videos, for which prior methods are ill-suited due to their focus on single-object scenes or discrete material classification with a fixed set of material prototypes. To address this, we propose MOSIV, a new framework that directly optimizes for continuous, per-object material parameters using a differentiable simulator guided by geometric objectives derived from video. We also present a new synthetic benchmark with contact-rich, multi-object interactions to facilitate evaluation. On this benchmark, MOSIV substantially improves grounding accuracy and long-horizon simulation fidelity over adapted baselines, establishing it as a strong baseline for this new task. Our analysis shows that object-level fine-grained supervision and geometry-aligned objectives are critical for stable optimization in these complex, multi-object settings. The source code and dataset will be released.

</details>


### [55] [StruVis: Enhancing Reasoning-based Text-to-Image Generation via Thinking with Structured Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06032)
*Yuanhuiyi Lyu,Kaiyu Lei,Ziqiao Weng,Xu Zheng,Lutao Jiang,Teng Li,Yangfu Li,Ziyuan Huang,Linfeng Zhang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StruVis是一个通过结构化视觉表示增强文本到图像生成推理的新框架，它使用文本形式的结构化视觉表示作为中间推理状态，避免了传统方法的视觉上下文缺失或计算成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成的推理框架存在两个主要问题：1）纯文本推理虽然计算高效但缺乏视觉上下文，容易忽略关键空间和视觉元素；2）文本-图像交错推理虽然能提供视觉参考但计算成本高，且受限于生成器的表示能力。需要一种既能有效感知视觉结构又计算高效的推理方法。

Method: 提出StruVis框架，使用文本形式的结构化视觉表示作为中间推理状态，让多模态大语言模型在纯文本推理过程中有效"感知"视觉结构。这是一个生成器无关的推理框架，可以与各种T2I生成器无缝集成。

Result: 在推理型文本到图像生成基准测试中取得显著性能提升：在T2I-ReasonBench上获得4.61%增益，在WISE上获得4%增益。

Conclusion: StruVis通过结构化视觉表示解锁了多模态大语言模型在文本到图像生成中的推理潜力，提供了一种既高效又能有效感知视觉结构的推理方法，可以无缝集成到各种T2I生成器中提升性能。

Abstract: Reasoning-based text-to-image (T2I) generation requires models to interpret complex prompts accurately. Existing reasoning frameworks can be broadly categorized into two types: (1) Text-Only Reasoning, which is computationally efficient but lacks access to visual context, often resulting in the omission of critical spatial and visual elements; and (2) Text-Image Interleaved Reasoning, which leverages a T2I generator to provide visual references during the reasoning process. While this approach enhances visual grounding, it incurs substantial computational costs and constrains the reasoning capacity of MLLMs to the representational limitations of the generator. To this end, we propose StruVis, a novel framework that enhances T2I generation through Thinking with Structured Vision. Instead of relying on intermediate image generation, StruVis employs text-based structured visual representations as intermediate reasoning states, thereby enabling the MLLM to effectively "perceive" visual structure within a purely text-based reasoning process. Powered by this, the reasoning potential for T2I generation of the MLLM is unlocked through structured-vision-guided reasoning. Additionally, as a generator-agnostic reasoning framework, our proposed StruVis can be seamlessly integrated with diverse T2I generators and efficiently enhance their performance in reasoning-based T2I generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StruVis achieves significant performance improvements on reasoning-based T2I benchmarks, e.g., a 4.61% gain on T2I-ReasonBench and a 4% gain on WISE.

</details>


### [56] [Occlusion-Aware SORT: Observing Occlusion for Robust Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06034)
*Chunjiang Li,Jianbo Ma,Li Shen,Yanru Chen,Liangyin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OA-SORT是一个无需训练的即插即用多目标跟踪框架，通过遮挡感知模块、遮挡感知偏移和偏差感知动量来解决2D MOT中的遮挡问题，在多个数据集上显著提升了跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D多目标跟踪面临部分遮挡导致的定位成本混淆问题，现有方法在处理遮挡时存在不足，需要更有效的遮挡感知机制来提升跟踪准确性和稳定性。

Method: 提出OA-SORT框架，包含三个核心组件：1)遮挡感知模块(OAM)分析物体遮挡状态，引入高斯地图减少背景影响；2)遮挡感知偏移(OAO)利用遮挡状态缓解成本混淆；3)偏差感知动量(BAM)抑制估计不稳定性。

Result: 在DanceTrack测试集上达到63.1% HOTA和64.2% IDF1；将框架集成到其他四个跟踪器后，HOTA和IDF1平均提升2.08%和3.05%，证明了遮挡感知的可重用性。

Conclusion: 遮挡处理在多目标跟踪中至关重要，OA-SORT框架通过创新的遮挡感知机制有效解决了遮挡引起的跟踪问题，且具有良好的可移植性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) involves analyzing object trajectories and counting the number of objects in video sequences. However, 2D MOT faces challenges due to positional cost confusion arising from partial occlusion. To address this issue, we present the novel Occlusion-Aware SORT (OA-SORT) framework, a plug-and-play and training-free framework that includes the Occlusion-Aware Module (OAM), the Occlusion-Aware Offset (OAO), and the Bias-Aware Momentum (BAM). Specifically, OAM analyzes the occlusion status of objects, where a Gaussian Map (GM) is introduced to reduce background influence. In contrast, OAO and BAM leverage the OAM-described occlusion status to mitigate cost confusion and suppress estimation instability. Comprehensive evaluations on the DanceTrack, SportsMOT, and MOT17 datasets demonstrate the importance of occlusion handling in MOT. On the DanceTrack test set, OA-SORT achieves 63.1% and 64.2% in HOTA and IDF1, respectively. Furthermore, integrating the Occlusion-Aware framework into the four additional trackers improves HOTA and IDF1 by an average of 2.08% and 3.05%, demonstrating the reusability of the occlusion awareness.

</details>


### [57] [Ensemble Learning with Sparse Hypercolumns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06036)
*Julia Dietlmeier,Vayangi Ganepola,Oluwabukola G. Adegboro,Mayug Maniparambil,Claudia Mazo,Noel E. O'Connor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过分层抽样和集成学习方法优化高维超列特征在医学图像分割中的应用，在极低样本量（N≤20）情况下，逻辑回归表现最佳，显著优于标准UNet基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生物视觉启发的高维超列特征可用于像素级图像分割，但实际应用较少，主要原因是处理密集超列的计算复杂度随训练集大小线性增长，限制了其在医学图像分析中的实用性。

Method: 采用分层抽样方法对VGG16生成的超列进行降维处理，研究集成学习（堆叠和投票集成）在稀疏超列上的性能，并与简单逻辑回归分类器在极低样本量情况下进行比较。

Result: 在脑肿瘤数据集上，当N=20且采用10%分层抽样率时，最佳平均Dice得分为0.66，相比标准多尺度UNet基线有24.53%的显著提升（p值=3.07e-11）。在极低样本量（N≤20）情况下，简单逻辑回归分类器表现最有效。

Conclusion: 通过分层抽样和集成学习可以有效优化超列特征的计算效率，在极低样本量的医学图像分割任务中，逻辑回归分类器比复杂集成方法更有效，显著优于标准UNet基线，避免了过拟合问题。

Abstract: Directly inspired by findings in biological vision, high-dimensional hypercolumns are feature vectors built by concatenating multi-scale activations of convolutional neural networks for a single image pixel location. Together with powerful classifiers, they can be used for image segmentation i.e. pixel classification. However, in practice, there are only very few works dedicated to the use of hypercolumns. One reason is the computational complexity of processing concatenated dense hypercolumns that grows linearly with the size $N$ of the training set. In this work, we address this challenge by applying stratified subsampling to the VGG16 based hypercolumns. Furthermore, we investigate the performance of ensemble learning on sparse hypercolumns. Our experiments on a brain tumor dataset show that stacking and voting ensembles deliver competitive performance, but in the extreme low-shot case of $N \leq 20$, a simple Logistic Regression classifier is the most effective method. For 10% stratified subsampling rate, our best average Dice score is 0.66 for $N=20$. This is a statistically significant improvement of 24.53% over the standard multi-scale UNet baseline ($p$-value = $[3.07e-11]$, Wilcoxon signed-rank test), which is less effective due to overfitting.

</details>


### [58] [FontUse: A Data-Centric Approach to Style- and Use-Case-Conditioned In-Image Typography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06038)
*Xia Xin,Yuki Endo,Yoshihiro Kanamori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种数据驱动的方法FontUse，通过构建大规模字体标注数据集和训练流程，解决了文本到图像模型中字体排版控制困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到图像模型在生成高质量图像方面表现出色，但在控制字体排版方面存在挑战：模型经常忽略或弱化用户请求的字体样式。字体排版对于许多实际应用（如海报、邀请函等）至关重要，但现有模型难以精确控制。

Method: 1. 构建FontUse数据集：包含约7万张图像，通过分割模型和多模态大语言模型自动标注用户友好提示、文本区域位置和OCR识别字符串。2. 提示设计：明确结合字体样式（如衬线体、手写体、优雅体）和使用场景（如婚礼邀请函、咖啡店菜单）。3. 微调现有生成模型：使用标注数据进行监督训练，无需修改模型架构。4. 评估指标：引入基于Long-CLIP的度量标准，衡量生成字体与请求属性的一致性。

Result: 实验表明，使用该管道训练的模型在多样化提示和布局下，生成的文本渲染比竞争基线更符合提示要求。模型能够一致地解释样式和使用场景条件作为文本提示。

Conclusion: 该研究通过数据中心的标注管道和训练方法，有效解决了文本到图像模型中字体排版控制的难题。FontUse数据集和评估指标为字体感知图像生成提供了有价值的资源，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image models can generate high-quality images from natural-language prompts, yet controlling typography remains challenging: requested typographic appearance is often ignored or only weakly followed. We address this limitation with a data-centric approach that trains image generation models using targeted supervision derived from a structured annotation pipeline specialized for typography. Our pipeline constructs a large-scale typography-focused dataset, FontUse, consisting of about 70K images annotated with user-friendly prompts, text-region locations, and OCR-recognized strings. The annotations are automatically produced using segmentation models and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). The prompts explicitly combine font styles (e.g., serif, script, elegant) and use cases (e.g., wedding invitations, coffee-shop menus), enabling intuitive specification even for novice users. Fine-tuning existing generators with these annotations allows them to consistently interpret style and use-case conditions as textual prompts without architectural modification. For evaluation, we introduce a Long-CLIP-based metric that measures alignment between generated typography and requested attributes. Experiments across diverse prompts and layouts show that models trained with our pipeline produce text renderings more consistent with prompts than competitive baselines. The source code for our annotation pipeline is available at https://github.com/xiaxinz/FontUSE.

</details>


### [59] [Learning to Generate via Understanding: Understanding-Driven Intrinsic Rewarding for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06043)
*Jiadong Pan,Liang Li,Yuxin Peng,Yu-Ming Tang,Shuohuan Wang,Yu Sun,Hua Wu,Qingming Huang,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GvU方法，利用统一多模态模型自身的理解能力来指导生成过程，通过自监督强化学习缩小理解与生成之间的能力差距


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型在视觉理解方面表现出色，但在生成能力上相对较弱，存在理解与生成过程的内在解耦问题。模型能够准确理解细粒度视觉细节，却难以从复杂文本提示生成语义连贯的图像。

Method: 提出GvU方法：1）基于token级别的内在文本-图像对齐奖励机制，让模型同时作为教师和学生；2）利用理解分支评估自身输出并指导生成；3）设计自监督强化学习框架，通过基于理解的内在奖励信号迭代提升生成质量，无需外部监督。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法显著提升了统一多模态模型的生成能力，同时增强了其细粒度视觉理解能力，有效缩小了视觉理解与生成之间的能力差距。

Conclusion: 通过利用统一多模态模型内部的理解能力来指导生成过程，可以显著提升生成质量，同时增强理解能力，实现理解与生成的协同提升。

Abstract: Recently, unified multimodal models (UMMs) have made remarkable progress in integrating visual understanding and generation, demonstrating strong potential for complex text-to-image (T2I) tasks. Despite their theoretical promise, a persistent capability gap exists: UMMs typically exhibit superior visual understanding but comparatively weaker generative capabilities. This discrepancy arises largely from the intrinsic decoupling between the understanding and generation processes. While a UMM can accurately interpret fine-grained visual details, it often struggles to produce semantically coherent images from complex textual prompts. To address this challenge, we explore UMMs' internal understanding capability to enhance generation quality. We propose a token-level intrinsic text-image alignment reward mechanism, GvU, enabling the UMM to act simultaneously as teacher and student: it evaluates its own outputs using the understanding branch to guide the generations accordingly. Building upon this, we design a self-supervised reinforcement learning framework, allowing UMMs to iteratively improve their generation quality through understanding-based intrinsic reward signals--without reliance on external supervision. Experimental results show that our method substantially boosts UMMs' generation, which in turn strengthens their fine-grained visual understanding, narrowing the capability gap between UMMs' visual understanding and generation.

</details>


### [60] [GenHOI: Towards Object-Consistent Hand-Object Interaction with Temporally Balanced and Spatially Selective Object Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06048)
*Xuan Huang,Mochu Xiang,Zhelun Shen,Jinbo Wu,Chenming Wu,Chen Zhao,Kaisiyuan Wang,Hang Zhou,Shanshan Liu,Haocheng Feng,Wei He,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenHOI是一种轻量级增强方法，通过时间平衡和空间选择性机制，将参考对象信息注入预训练视频生成模型，显著提升手-物体交互视频合成的物理合理性和对象一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前手-物体交互（HOI）视频合成面临两大挑战：现有HOI重演方法缺乏对复杂野外场景的泛化能力，而通用视频编辑模型在处理HOI特定问题（如对象外观不一致）时表现不佳。需要一种既能保持广泛鲁棒性又能解决HOI特定问题的方法。

Method: 提出GenHOI框架，包含两个核心组件：1）时间平衡机制：采用Head-Sliding RoPE，为参考令牌分配头部特定的时间偏移，均匀分布其影响力并缓解3D RoPE的时间衰减；2）空间选择性机制：设计两级空间注意力门，将对象条件注意力集中在HOI区域，并自适应调整强度，保持背景真实性的同时增强交互保真度。

Result: 在未见过的野外场景上进行广泛定性和定量评估，GenHOI在物理合理性、对象一致性等方面显著优于最先进的HOI重演方法和通用视频编辑方法。

Conclusion: GenHOI通过轻量级增强预训练视频生成模型，有效解决了手-物体交互视频合成中的关键挑战，实现了更好的泛化能力和交互保真度，为复杂野外场景的HOI视频生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Hand-Object Interaction (HOI) remains a core challenge in digital human video synthesis, where models must generate physically plausible contact and preserve object identity across frames. Although recent HOI reenactment approaches have achieved progress, they are typically trained and evaluated in-domain and fail to generalize to complex, in-the-wild scenarios. In contrast, all-in-one video editing models exhibit broader robustness but still struggle with HOI-specific issues such as inconsistent object appearance. In this paper, we present GenHOI, a lightweight augmentation to pretrained video generation models that injects reference-object information in a temporally balanced and spatially selective manner. For temporal balancing, we propose Head-Sliding RoPE, which assigns head-specific temporal offsets to reference tokens, distributing their influence evenly across frames and mitigating the temporal decay of 3D RoPE to improve long-range object consistency. For spatial selectivity, we design a two-level spatial attention gate that concentrates object-conditioned attention on HOI regions and adaptively scales its strength, preserving background realism while enhancing interaction fidelity. Extensive qualitative and quantitative evaluations on unseen, in-the-wild scenes demonstrate that GenHOI significantly outperforms state-of-the-art HOI reenactment and all-in-one video editing methods. Project page: https://xuanhuang0.github.io/GenHOI/

</details>


### [61] [Devil is in Narrow Policy: Unleashing Exploration in Driving VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06049)
*Canyu Chen,Yuguang Yang,Zhewen Tan,Yizhi Wang,Ruiyi Zhan,Haiyan Liu,Xuanyao Mao,Jason Bao,Xinyue Tang,Linlin Yang,Bingchuan Sun,Yan Wang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Curious-VLA框架通过两阶段设计解决自动驾驶VLA模型的探索限制问题：IL阶段使用可行轨迹扩展和归一化表示，RL阶段采用自适应多样性采样和跨度驾驶奖励，在Navsim基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现自动驾驶VLA模型存在"窄策略限制"问题：模仿学习阶段会限制探索能力，导致后续强化学习阶段因反馈多样性不足而提前饱和，无法充分发挥模型潜力。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：1) IL阶段：引入可行轨迹扩展策略生成多个物理有效轨迹，使用步进归一化轨迹表示适应多样化数据；2) RL阶段：提出自适应多样性感知采样优先选择高多样性样本，引入跨度驾驶奖励和焦点风格加权来扩大奖励值范围，提高对驾驶质量的敏感性。

Result: 在Navsim基准测试中，Curious-VLA取得SOTA结果：PDMS 90.3，EPDMS 85.4，Best-of-N PDMS达到94.8，证明该框架能有效释放VLA模型的探索潜力。

Conclusion: Curious-VLA框架通过解决探索-利用困境，成功缓解了自动驾驶VLA模型的窄策略限制，在保持物理有效性的同时增强了模型的探索能力和性能表现。

Abstract: We identify a fundamental Narrow Policy limitation undermining the performance of autonomous VLA models, where driving Imitation Learning (IL) tends to collapse exploration and limit the potential of subsequent Reinforcement Learning (RL) stages, which often saturate prematurely due to insufficient feedback diversity. Thereby, we propose Curious-VLA, a framework that alleviates the exploit-explore dilemma through a two-stage design. During IL, we introduce a Feasible Trajectory Expansion (FTE) strategy to generate multiple physically valid trajectories and a step-wise normalized trajectory representation to adapt this diverse data. In the RL stage, we present Adaptive Diversity-Aware Sampling (ADAS) that prioritizes high-diversity samples and introduce Spanning Driving Reward (SDR) with a focal style weighting to amplify reward's value span for improving sensitivity to driving quality. On the Navsim benchmark, Curious-VLA achieves SoTA results (PDMS 90.3, EPDMS 85.4) and a Best-of-N PDMS of 94.8, demonstrating its effectiveness in unlocking the exploratory potential of VLA models. Code: https://github.com/Mashiroln/curious_vla.git.

</details>


### [62] [Probing Visual Concepts in Lightweight Vision-Language Models for Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06054)
*Nikos Theodoridis,Reenu Mohandas,Ganesh Sistu,Anthony Scanlan,Ciarán Eising,Tim Brophy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究通过分析视觉语言模型在自动驾驶场景中的中间激活，发现某些视觉概念（如物体存在）是线性编码的，而其他空间概念（如物体朝向）仅隐式编码，并识别出感知失败和认知失败两种故障模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉语言模型在自动驾驶应用中越来越普遍，但它们经常在简单但关键的视觉问题上失败，且失败原因尚不明确。本研究旨在通过分析模型中间激活来理解这些失败的根本原因。

Method: 创建仅针对特定视觉概念不同的反事实图像集，使用四种最先进的视觉语言模型的激活训练线性探针，评估视觉概念是否被线性编码，并分析信息流动瓶颈。

Result: 发现物体存在等概念被明确线性编码，而物体朝向等空间概念仅通过视觉编码器保留的空间结构隐式编码。识别出两种故障模式：感知失败（所需视觉信息未线性编码）和认知失败（信息存在但未正确与语言语义对齐）。物体距离增加会快速降低相应视觉概念的线性可分性。

Conclusion: 研究提高了对视觉语言模型在自动驾驶相关简单视觉任务中失败案例的理解，揭示了信息编码的局限性，并为改进模型在关键场景中的表现提供了见解。

Abstract: The use of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in automated driving applications is becoming increasingly common, with the aim of leveraging their reasoning and generalisation capabilities to handle long tail scenarios. However, these models often fail on simple visual questions that are highly relevant to automated driving, and the reasons behind these failures remain poorly understood. In this work, we examine the intermediate activations of VLMs and assess the extent to which specific visual concepts are linearly encoded, with the goal of identifying bottlenecks in the flow of visual information. Specifically, we create counterfactual image sets that differ only in a targeted visual concept and then train linear probes to distinguish between them using the activations of four state-of-the-art (SOTA) VLMs. Our results show that concepts such as the presence of an object or agent in a scene are explicitly and linearly encoded, whereas other spatial visual concepts, such as the orientation of an object or agent, are only implicitly encoded by the spatial structure retained by the vision encoder. In parallel, we observe that in certain cases, even when a concept is linearly encoded in the model's activations, the model still fails to answer correctly. This leads us to identify two failure modes. The first is perceptual failure, where the visual information required to answer a question is not linearly encoded in the model's activations. The second is cognitive failure, where the visual information is present but the model fails to align it correctly with language semantics. Finally, we show that increasing the distance of the object in question quickly degrades the linear separability of the corresponding visual concept. Overall, our findings improve our understanding of failure cases in VLMs on simple visual tasks that are highly relevant to automated driving.

</details>


### [63] [TempoSyncDiff: Distilled Temporally-Consistent Diffusion for Low-Latency Audio-Driven Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06057)
*Soumya Mazumdar,Vineet Kumar Rakesh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TempoSyncDiff是一个基于参考条件的潜在扩散框架，通过师生蒸馏实现高效音频驱动说话头生成，减少推理步骤、提高稳定性，并解决身份漂移和闪烁问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型在说话头生成中存在高推理延迟、时间不稳定性（闪烁和身份漂移）以及在挑战性语音条件下视听对齐不完美的问题，需要开发更实用的解决方案。

Method: 采用参考条件的潜在扩散框架，通过师生蒸馏方法：使用标准噪声预测目标训练的扩散教师指导轻量级学生去噪器，减少推理步骤；结合身份锚定和时间正则化减少身份漂移和闪烁；使用基于视位的音频条件控制唇部运动。

Result: 在LRS3数据集上的实验显示，蒸馏扩散模型能保留强教师的重建行为，同时实现显著降低的延迟推理；包括CPU和边缘计算的延迟测量表明边缘部署的可行性。

Conclusion: 蒸馏扩散模型可以在保持教师模型重建能力的同时实现低延迟推理，这是向受限计算环境下实用扩散基说话头生成迈出的初步步骤。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently advanced photorealistic human synthesis, although practical talking-head generation (THG) remains constrained by high inference latency, temporal instability such as flicker and identity drift, and imperfect audio-visual alignment under challenging speech conditions. This paper introduces TempoSyncDiff, a reference-conditioned latent diffusion framework that explores few-step inference for efficient audio-driven talking-head generation. The approach adopts a teacher-student distillation formulation in which a diffusion teacher trained with a standard noise prediction objective guides a lightweight student denoiser capable of operating with significantly fewer inference steps to improve generation stability. The framework incorporates identity anchoring and temporal regularization designed to mitigate identity drift and frame-to-frame flicker during synthesis, while viseme-based audio conditioning provides coarse lip motion control. Experiments on the LRS3 dataset report denoising-stage component-level metrics relative to VAE reconstructions and preliminary latency characterization, including CPU-only and edge computing measurements and feasibility estimates for edge deployment. The results suggest that distilled diffusion models can retain much of the reconstruction behaviour of a stronger teacher while enabling substantially lower latency inference. The study is positioned as an initial step toward practical diffusion-based talking-head generation under constrained computational settings. GitHub: https://mazumdarsoumya.github.io/TempoSyncDiff

</details>


### [64] [Text-Driven Emotionally Continuous Talking Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06071)
*Hao Yang,Yanyan Zhao,Tian Zheng,Hongbo Zhang,Bichen Wang,Di Wu,Xing Fu,Xuda Zhi,Yongbo Huang,Hao He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出情感连续说话人脸生成任务(EC-TFG)，通过TIE-TFG模型实现文本驱动下动态情感变化的自然面部表情生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话人脸生成方法虽然能生成自然的面部运动，但通常只能表达固定目标情感，缺乏人类在传达信息时表现出的连续变化和自然表情能力

Method: 提出TIE-TFG模型，采用时间密集型情感波动建模技术，通过输入文本和情感描述生成对应情感变化序列，驱动合成视频中的连续面部表情变化

Result: 广泛评估表明该方法在产生平滑情感过渡、保持高质量视觉效果和运动真实性方面表现优异，能够适应多种情感状态

Conclusion: 成功实现了情感连续说话人脸生成，解决了传统方法情感表达固定的局限性，为创造更自然、情感丰富的数字人脸提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Talking Face Generation (TFG) strives to create realistic and emotionally expressive digital faces. While previous TFG works have mastered the creation of naturalistic facial movements, they typically express a fixed target emotion in synthetic videos and lack the ability to exhibit continuously changing and natural expressions like humans do when conveying information. To synthesize realistic videos, we propose a novel task called Emotionally Continuous Talking Face Generation (EC-TFG), which takes a text segment and an emotion description with varying emotions as driving data, aiming to generate a video where the person speaks the text while reflecting the emotional changes within the description. Alongside this, we introduce a customized model, i.e., Temporal-Intensive Emotion Modulated Talking Face Generation (TIE-TFG), which innovatively manages dynamic emotional variations by employing Temporal-Intensive Emotion Fluctuation Modeling, allowing it to provide emotion variation sequences corresponding to the input text to drive continuous facial expression changes in synthesized videos. Extensive evaluations demonstrate our method's exceptional ability to produce smooth emotion transitions and uphold high-quality visuals and motion authenticity across diverse emotional states.

</details>


### [65] [FedARKS: Federated Aggregation via Robust and Discriminative Knowledge Selection and Integration for Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06122)
*Xin Xu,Binchang Ma,Zhixi Yu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedARKS框架通过鲁棒知识提取和知识选择机制，解决联邦域泛化行人重识别中全局特征不足和参数平均忽略客户端差异的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦域泛化行人重识别方法依赖全局特征表示和简单平均聚合，存在两个局限：1) 仅用全局特征难以捕捉细粒度域不变局部细节；2) 均匀参数平均忽略客户端在鲁棒特征提取能力上的差异，稀释高质量客户端贡献

Method: 提出FedARKS框架，包含两个机制：RK（鲁棒知识）用于提取细粒度局部特征，KS（知识选择）根据客户端特征提取能力差异进行选择性聚合

Result: 论文声称FedARKS能够提升模型在未见域上的泛化能力，同时保护客户端数据隐私

Conclusion: FedARKS通过结合鲁棒知识提取和选择性知识集成，有效解决了联邦域泛化行人重识别中的特征表示和聚合问题

Abstract: The application of federated domain generalization in person re-identification (FedDG-ReID) aims to enhance the model's generalization ability in unseen domains while protecting client data privacy. However, existing mainstream methods typically rely on global feature representations and simple averaging operations for model aggregation, leading to two limitations in domain generalization: (1) Using only global features makes it difficult to capture subtle, domain-invariant local details (such as accessories or textures); (2) Uniform parameter averaging treats all clients as equivalent, ignoring their differences in robust feature extraction capabilities, thereby diluting the contributions of high quality clients. To address these issues, we propose a novel federated learning framework, Federated Aggregation via Robust and Discriminative Knowledge Selection and Integration (FedARKS), comprising two mechanisms: RK (Robust Knowledge) and KS (Knowledge Selection).

</details>


### [66] [Cross-Resolution Distribution Matching for Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06136)
*Feiyang Chen,Hongpeng Pan,Haonan Xu,Xinyu Duan,Yang Yang,Zhefeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RMD提出了一种跨分辨率分布匹配蒸馏框架，通过logSNR曲线划分时间步区间、分布匹配和噪声重注入机制，在保持高保真度的同时显著加速扩散模型推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散蒸馏方法受限于去噪过程，时间步减少已基本饱和；部分时间步低分辨率生成虽然能加速推理，但存在跨分辨率分布差距导致质量下降的问题

Method: 1) 使用logSNR曲线为每个分辨率划分时间步区间；2) 引入基于logSNR的映射补偿分辨率引起的偏移；3) 沿分辨率轨迹进行分布匹配，缩小低分辨率生成器分布与教师高分辨率分布之间的差距；4) 在上采样过程中加入预测噪声重注入机制稳定训练

Result: RMD在保持高视觉保真度的同时显著加速推理：SDXL上达到33.4倍加速，Wan2.1-14B上达到25.6倍加速；定量和定性结果均显示RMD能保持高质量生成

Conclusion: RMD通过跨分辨率分布匹配有效解决了现有扩散蒸馏方法的局限性，实现了高保真度、少步数的多分辨率级联推理加速，为扩散模型的实际应用提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Diffusion distillation is central to accelerating image and video generation, yet existing methods are fundamentally limited by the denoising process, where step reduction has largely saturated. Partial timestep low-resolution generation can further accelerate inference, but it suffers noticeable quality degradation due to cross-resolution distribution gaps. We propose Cross-Resolution Distribution Matching Distillation (RMD), a novel distillation framework that bridges cross-resolution distribution gaps for high-fidelity, few-step multi-resolution cascaded inference. Specifically, RMD divides the timestep intervals for each resolution using logarithmic signal-to-noise ratio (logSNR) curves, and introduces logSNR-based mapping to compensate for resolution-induced shifts. Distribution matching is conducted along resolution trajectories to reduce the gap between low-resolution generator distributions and the teacher's high-resolution distribution. In addition, a predicted-noise re-injection mechanism is incorporated during upsampling to stabilize training and improve synthesis quality. Quantitative and qualitative results show that RMD preserves high-fidelity generation while accelerating inference across various backbones. Notably, RMD achieves up to 33.4X speedup on SDXL and 25.6X on Wan2.1-14B, while preserving high visual fidelity.

</details>


### [67] [Place-it-R1: Unlocking Environment-aware Reasoning Potential of MLLM for Video Object Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06140)
*Bohai Gu,Taiyi Wu,Dazhao Du,Jian Liu,Shuai Yang,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Place-it-R1是一个基于多模态大语言模型的视频物体插入框架，通过物理因果推理实现环境感知的视频编辑，提供灵活性和保真度两种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑技术主要关注视觉保真度而非物理因果关系，导致插入的物体与环境物理不一致。需要开发能够理解物理场景并进行环境感知推理的视频对象插入方法。

Method: 采用Think-then-Place范式，利用MLLM的链式思维推理协调视频扩散模型。关键创新包括：1) MLLM进行物理场景理解和交互推理，生成环境感知的CoT令牌和有效插入区域；2) MLLM引导的空间直接偏好优化，通过反馈评分实现视觉自然性；3) 推理时的迭代细化循环，形成闭环系统逐步提升编辑质量。

Result: 实验表明Place-it-R1相比现有最先进解决方案和商业模型，能够实现物理一致性的视频物体插入。框架提供两种用户可选模式：增强物理合理性的灵活性模式（允许环境修改）和保持场景完整性的保真度模式。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了MLLM在环境感知推理方面的潜力，通过认知推理与生成执行的结合，实现了物理一致性的视频对象插入，为用户提供了在合理性与保真度之间的明确控制。

Abstract: Modern video editing techniques have achieved high visual fidelity when inserting video objects. However, they focus on optimizing visual fidelity rather than physical causality, leading to edits that are physically inconsistent with their environment. In this work, we present Place-it-R$1$, an end-to-end framework for video object insertion that unlocks the environment-aware reasoning potential of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Our framework leverages the Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning of MLLMs to orchestrate video diffusion, following a Think-then-Place paradigm. To bridge cognitive reasoning and generative execution, we introduce three key innovations: First, MLLM performs physical scene understanding and interaction reasoning, generating environment-aware chain-of-thought tokens and inferring valid insertion regions to explicitly guide the diffusion toward physically plausible insertion. Then, we introduce MLLM-guided Spatial Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), where diffusion outputs are fed back to the MLLM for scoring, enabling visual naturalness. During inference, the MLLM iteratively triggers refinement cycles and elicits adaptive adjustments from the diffusion model, forming a closed-loop that progressively enhances editing quality. Furthermore, we provide two user-selectable modes: a plausibility-oriented flexible mode that permits environment modifications (\eg, generating support structures) to enhance physical plausibility, and a fidelity-oriented standard mode that preserves scene integrity for maximum fidelity, offering users explicit control over the plausibility-fidelity trade-off. Extensive experiments demonstrate Place-it-R1 achieves physically-coherent video object insertion compared with state-of-the-art solutions and commercial models.

</details>


### [68] [Spatial Colour Mixing Illusions as a Perception Stress Test for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06141)
*Nicoleta-Nina Basoc,Adrian Cosma,Emilian Radoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究了视觉语言模型在结构化颜色扭曲下的系统感知弱点，发现即使场景对人类仍可识别，VLM的准确率会急剧下降，而人类表现远优于VLM，通过人类启发的预处理可以部分恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在基准测试中表现良好，但在面对结构化颜色扭曲时会出现系统性的感知弱点，即使场景对人类仍然容易识别，模型也会做出自信但无意义的预测。研究者希望量化这种人类与VLM之间的感知差距。

Method: 提出空间颜色混合方法，使用程序化生成的颜色扭曲家族（包括RGB和Ostwald颜色系统），设计了8种空间颜色混合变体。评估了9个VLM（来自3个模型家族）在4个数据集上的表现。同时进行了包含61名参与者的人类研究，比较人类和VLM在相同扭曲下的表现差异。

Result: 随着扭曲程度的增加，所有VLM的准确率急剧下降，扩大语言模型规模并不能可靠地缓解这种失败。人类在相同扭曲下显著优于VLM。简单的、受人类启发的预处理步骤能够为几种扭曲类型恢复相当一部分性能。

Conclusion: VLM存在系统性的感知弱点，在面对结构化颜色扭曲时表现脆弱。人类启发的预处理和工具使用可以作为提高VLM鲁棒性的实用策略，这为开发更感知感知的预处理方法提供了动机。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong benchmark results, yet can exhibit systematic perceptual weaknesses: structured, large changes to pixel values can cause confident yet nonsensical predictions, even when the underlying scene remains easily recognizable to humans. We study this gap using Spatial Colour Mixing, a programmatic family of colour distortions that overlays structured patterns (in both RGB and Ostwald colour systems) onto natural images. We introduce a framework of eight spatial colour mixing variants and evaluate nine VLMs across three model families on four datasets. Across models and datasets, accuracy degrades sharply with increasing distortion, and scaling the language model does not reliably mitigate the failure. In a human study with 61 participants on an animal recognition dataset, humans substantially outperform VLMs under the same distortions. Finally, we show that a simple human-inspired preprocessing step recovers a meaningful portion of performance for several distortion types, motivating perception-aware preprocessing and tool-use as practical strategies for improving VLM robustness.

</details>


### [69] [Longitudinal NSCLC Treatment Progression via Multimodal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06147)
*Massimiliano Mantegna,Elena Mulero Ayllón,Alice Natalina Caragliano,Francesco Di Feola,Claudia Tacconi,Michele Fiore,Edy Ippolito,Carlo Greco,Sara Ramella,Philippe C. Cattin,Paolo Soda,Matteo Tortora,Valerio Guarrasi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出虚拟治疗框架，将非小细胞肺癌放疗期间肿瘤演化建模为剂量感知的多模态条件图像翻译问题，用于合成治疗引起的解剖变化随访CT图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测放疗期间肿瘤演化是临床关键挑战，特别是当纵向变化同时受解剖结构和治疗影响时。现有方法难以准确模拟放疗引起的肿瘤变化轨迹。

Method: 提出虚拟治疗框架，将NSCLC进展建模为剂量感知的多模态条件图像翻译问题。基于CT扫描、基线临床变量和特定辐射剂量增量，合成反映治疗诱导解剖变化的随访CT图像。在222名III期NSCLC患者的895个CT扫描数据集上评估，生成过程以递送剂量增量、人口统计学和肿瘤相关临床变量为条件。比较了GAN和扩散模型在2D和2.5D配置下的性能。

Result: 定量和定性结果表明，扩散模型更一致地从多模态、剂量感知条件中受益，相比GAN基线产生更稳定、解剖学上更合理的肿瘤演化轨迹。支持VT作为NSCLC计算机内治疗监测和自适应放疗研究工具的潜力。

Conclusion: 虚拟治疗框架成功将NSCLC放疗期间肿瘤演化建模为剂量感知多模态条件图像翻译问题。扩散模型在该任务中表现优于GAN，为计算机内治疗监测和自适应放疗研究提供了有前景的工具。

Abstract: Predicting tumor evolution during radiotherapy is a clinically critical challenge, particularly when longitudinal changes are driven by both anatomy and treatment. In this work, we introduce a Virtual Treatment (VT) framework that formulates non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) progression as a dose-aware multimodal conditional image-to-image translation problem. Given a CT scan, baseline clinical variables, and a specified radiation dose increment, VT aims to synthesize plausible follow-up CT images reflecting treatment-induced anatomical changes. We evaluate the proposed framework on a longitudinal dataset of 222 stage III NSCLC patients, comprising 895 CT scans acquired during radiotherapy under irregular clinical schedules. The generative process is conditioned on delivered dose increments together with demographic and tumor-related clinical variables. Representative GAN-based and diffusion-based models are benchmarked across 2D and 2.5D configurations. Quantitative and qualitative results indicate that diffusion-based models benefit more consistently from multimodal, dose-aware conditioning and produce more stable and anatomically plausible tumor evolution trajectories than GAN-based baselines, supporting the potential of VT as a tool for in-silico treatment monitoring and adaptive radiotherapy research in NSCLC.

</details>


### [70] [VLM-RobustBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robustness of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06148)
*Rohit Saxena,Alessandro Suglia,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLM-RobustBench是一个评估视觉语言模型在现实世界图像失真下鲁棒性的基准，涵盖49种增强类型，133种损坏设置。研究发现当前VLM在语义理解上强大但在空间变换上脆弱，低严重度的空间扰动比视觉上严重的光度损坏影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在标准高质量数据集上表现良好，但对其在现实世界图像失真下的性能理解不足。需要系统评估VLM对各种图像扰动的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建VLM-RobustBench基准，涵盖49种增强类型（噪声、模糊、天气、数字和几何扰动），在分级严重度（低/中/高）和二元变换下评估，产生133种损坏设置。在四个VLM家族（Qwen、InternVL、Molmo、Gemma）上使用两个互补基准进行评估：MMBench（视觉基础）和MMMU-Pro（推理导向）。

Result: 视觉严重度是性能下降的弱预测因子：低严重度的空间扰动通常比视觉上严重的光度损坏更能降低性能。低严重度的glass_blur平均降低MMBench准确率约8个百分点，最大的性能下降来自重采样和几何失真（如上采样、弹性变换），可达34个百分点。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在语义理解上强大但在空间变换上脆弱，需要定义新的鲁棒性评估协议和训练机制，强调重采样和几何不变性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on standard, high-quality datasets, but we still do not fully understand how they perform under real-world image distortions. We present VLM-RobustBench, a benchmark spanning 49 augmentation types across noise, blur, weather, digital, and geometric perturbations, evaluated under graded severities (low/mid/high) and binary transforms, yielding 133 corrupted settings. We evaluate VLMs from four families (Qwen, InternVL, Molmo, Gemma) on two complementary benchmarks: MMBench (visually grounded) and MMMU-Pro (reasoning-oriented). Our results reveal that visual severity is a weak predictor of difficulty: low-severity spatial perturbations often degrade performance more than visually severe photometric corruptions. In particular, low-severity glass_blur reduces MMBench accuracy by about 8 pp on average across models, while the largest drops arise from resampling and geometric distortions (e.g., upsample, elastic_transform), reaching up to 34 pp. Overall, our findings suggest current VLMs are semantically strong but spatially fragile, motivating the definition of novel robustness evaluation protocols and training regimes that emphasize resampling and geometric invariances.

</details>


### [71] [Reflective Flow Sampling Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06165)
*Zikai Zhou,Muyao Wang,Shitong Shao,Lichen Bai,Haoyi Xiong,Bo Han,Zeke Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RF-Sampling，一种专门为流匹配模型设计的无需训练推理增强框架，通过理论推导证明其能隐式执行文本-图像对齐分数的梯度上升，显著提升生成质量和提示对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到图像生成需求增长迅速，流匹配模型（如FLUX）取得了显著进展。然而，现有的推理时增强策略主要适用于传统扩散模型，在流模型上效果不佳。需要为流模型（特别是CFG蒸馏变体）设计专门的推理增强方法。

Method: 提出Reflective Flow Sampling (RF-Sampling)，这是一个理论基础的无需训练推理增强框架。通过线性组合文本表示并与流反转结合，使模型能够探索与输入提示更一致的噪声空间。方法基于形式化推导，证明其隐式执行文本-图像对齐分数的梯度上升。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，RF-Sampling能持续提升生成质量和提示对齐。此外，RF-Sampling是首个在FLUX上展示一定程度测试时缩放能力的推理增强方法。

Conclusion: RF-Sampling成功填补了流模型推理增强的空白，为流匹配模型（特别是CFG蒸馏变体）提供了有效的无需训练增强方案，在理论和实践上都取得了显著成果。

Abstract: The growing demand for text-to-image generation has led to rapid advances in generative modeling. Recently, text-to-image diffusion models trained with flow matching algorithms, such as FLUX, have achieved remarkable progress and emerged as strong alternatives to conventional diffusion models. At the same time, inference-time enhancement strategies have been shown to improve the generation quality and text-prompt alignment of text-to-image diffusion models. However, these techniques are mainly applicable to conventional diffusion models and usually fail to perform well on flow models. To bridge this gap, we propose Reflective Flow Sampling (RF-Sampling), a theoretically-grounded and training-free inference enhancement framework explicitly designed for flow models, especially for the CFG-distilled variants (i.e., models distilled from CFG guidance techniques), like FLUX. Departing from heuristic interpretations, we provide a formal derivation proving that RF-Sampling implicitly performs gradient ascent on the text-image alignment score. By leveraging a linear combination of textual representations and integrating them with flow inversion, RF-Sampling allows the model to explore noise spaces that are more consistent with the input prompt. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that RF-Sampling consistently improves both generation quality and prompt alignment. Moreover, RF-Sampling is also the first inference enhancement method that can exhibit test-time scaling ability to some extent on FLUX.

</details>


### [72] [FreeOcc: Training-free Panoptic Occupancy Prediction via Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06166)
*Andrew Caunes,Thierry Chateau,Vincent Fremont*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeOcc是一个无需训练、基于预训练基础模型的3D语义和全景占据预测框架，通过多视角图像恢复语义和几何信息，在未见环境中实现场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于相机的3D占据预测方法通常需要昂贵的密集3D监督数据或需要在目标域数据上训练模型，这限制了在未见环境中的部署。需要一种无需训练、能够泛化到新环境的解决方案。

Method: 1. 使用可提示的基础分割模型提取每视角的全景先验，通过提示到分类规则获取语义信息；2. 使用重建基础模型恢复度量3D点云；3. 通过深度和置信度感知过滤将可靠标签提升到3D空间；4. 随时间融合并体素化，使用确定性细化堆栈；5. 对于全景占据，通过拟合和合并当前视角的3D边界框候选来恢复实例，实现无需学习3D模型的实例感知占据。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes数据集上：1. 无需训练达到16.9 mIoU和16.5 RayIoU，与最先进的弱监督方法相当；2. 作为伪标签生成管道训练下游模型时达到21.1 RayIoU，超越之前的弱监督基线；3. 在无需训练和弱监督全景占据预测上分别达到3.1 RayPQ和3.9 RayPQ，建立了新的基准。

Conclusion: FreeOcc展示了基于基础模型的感知是实现无需训练的3D场景理解的实用途径，通过利用预训练模型的能力，避免了昂贵的3D监督和特定域训练需求，为在未见环境中的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Semantic and panoptic occupancy prediction for road scene analysis provides a dense 3D representation of the ego vehicle's surroundings. Current camera-only approaches typically rely on costly dense 3D supervision or require training models on data from the target domain, limiting deployment in unseen environments. We propose FreeOcc, a training-free pipeline that leverages pretrained foundation models to recover both semantics and geometry from multi-view images. FreeOcc extracts per-view panoptic priors with a promptable foundation segmentation model and prompt-to-taxonomy rules, and reconstructs metric 3D points with a reconstruction foundation model. Depth- and confidence- aware filtering lifts reliable labels into 3D, which are fused over time and voxelized with a deterministic refinement stack. For panoptic occupancy, instances are recovered by fitting and merging robust current-view 3D box candidates, enabling instance-aware occupancy without any learned 3D model. On Occ3D-nuScenes, FreeOcc achieves 16.9 mIoU and 16.5 RayIoU train-free, on par with state-of-the-art weakly supervised methods. When employed as a pseudo-label generation pipeline for training downstream models, it achieves 21.1 RayIoU, surpassing the previous state-of-the-art weakly supervised baseline. Furthermore, FreeOcc sets new baselines for both train-free and weakly supervised panoptic occupancy prediction, achieving 3.1 RayPQ and 3.9 RayPQ, respectively. These results highlight foundation-model-driven perception as a practical route to training-free 3D scene understanding.

</details>


### [73] [A Semi-Supervised Framework for Breast Ultrasound Segmentation with Training-Free Pseudo-Label Generation and Label Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06167)
*Ruili Li,Jiayi Ding,Ruiyu Li,Yilun Jin,Shiwen Ge,Yuwen Zeng,Xiaoyong Zhang,Eichi Takaya,Jan Vrba,Noriyasu Homma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用于乳腺超声图像分割的半监督框架，通过免训练的伪标签生成和标签精炼，利用视觉语言模型生成结构一致的伪标签，在仅有2.5%标注数据下达到全监督模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督学习在极有限标注下存在伪标签不稳定问题，导致监督不准确和性能下降。现有视觉语言模型在医学图像上的效果有限，因为领域特定提示难以迁移。

Method: 提出免训练的伪标签生成和标签精炼框架：1) 利用简单外观描述实现自然图像到医学图像的结构迁移，生成结构一致的伪标签；2) 用伪标签预热静态教师模型捕获病灶全局结构先验；3) 结合指数移动平均教师，引入不确定性熵加权融合和自适应不确定性引导的反向对比学习来改善边界判别。

Result: 在四个乳腺超声数据集上，仅使用2.5%标注数据即达到与全监督模型相当的性能，显著优于现有半监督方法。该范式可扩展到其他成像模态或疾病，仅需全局外观描述即可获得可靠的伪监督。

Conclusion: 提出的半监督框架通过视觉语言模型实现跨域结构迁移，在极有限标注下实现有效的医学图像分割，为有限标注下的可扩展半监督医学图像分割提供了新范式。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for breast ultrasound (BUS) image segmentation, but it often suffers from unstable pseudo labels under extremely limited annotations, leading to inaccurate supervision and degraded performance. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) provide a new opportunity for pseudo-label generation, yet their effectiveness on BUS images remains limited because domain-specific prompts are difficult to transfer.
  To address this issue, we propose a semi-supervised framework with training-free pseudo-label generation and label refinement. By leveraging simple appearance-based descriptions (e.g., dark oval), our method enables cross-domain structural transfer between natural and medical images, allowing VLMs to generate structurally consistent pseudo labels. These pseudo labels are used to warm up a static teacher that captures global structural priors of breast lesions. Combined with an exponential moving average teacher, we further introduce uncertainty entropy weighted fusion and adaptive uncertainty-guided reverse contrastive learning to improve boundary discrimination.
  Experiments on four BUS datasets demonstrate that our method achieves performance comparable to fully supervised models even with only 2.5% labeled data, significantly outperforming existing SSL approaches. Moreover, the proposed paradigm is readily extensible: for other imaging modalities or diseases, only a global appearance description is required to obtain reliable pseudo supervision, enabling scalable semi-supervised medical image segmentation under limited annotations.

</details>


### [74] [JOPP-3D: Joint Open Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation on Point Clouds and Panoramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06168)
*Sandeep Inuganti,Hideaki Kanayama,Kanta Shimizu,Mahdi Chamseddine,Soichiro Yokota,Didier Stricker,Jason Rambach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JOPP-3D是一个开放词汇语义分割框架，通过联合利用全景图像和点云数据，实现语言驱动的场景理解，在开放和封闭词汇的2D和3D语义分割上显著优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视觉模态（如3D点云和全景图像）的语义分割面临挑战，主要由于标注数据稀缺和固定标签模型适应性有限。需要一种能够利用自然语言查询进行场景理解的方法。

Method: 将RGB-D全景图像转换为对应的切向透视图像和3D点云，利用这些模态提取和对齐基础视觉语言特征，通过自然语言查询在两个输入模态上生成语义掩码。

Result: 在Stanford-2D-3D-s和ToF-360数据集上的实验评估表明，JOPP-3D能够跨全景和3D域生成连贯且语义有意义的分割，在开放和封闭词汇的2D和3D语义分割方面相比SOTA有显著改进。

Conclusion: JOPP-3D框架成功实现了跨模态的开放词汇语义分割，通过联合利用全景和点云数据，为语言驱动的场景理解提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation across visual modalities such as 3D point clouds and panoramic images remains a challenging task, primarily due to the scarcity of annotated data and the limited adaptability of fixed-label models. In this paper, we present JOPP-3D, an open-vocabulary semantic segmentation framework that jointly leverages panoramic and point cloud data to enable language-driven scene understanding. We convert RGB-D panoramic images into their corresponding tangential perspective images and 3D point clouds, then use these modalities to extract and align foundational vision-language features. This allows natural language querying to generate semantic masks on both input modalities. Experimental evaluation on the Stanford-2D-3D-s and ToF-360 datasets demonstrates the capability of JOPP-3D to produce coherent and semantically meaningful segmentations across panoramic and 3D domains. Our proposed method achieves a significant improvement compared to the SOTA in open and closed vocabulary 2D and 3D semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [75] [Optimizing 3D Diffusion Models for Medical Imaging via Multi-Scale Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06173)
*Yueying Tian,Xudong Han,Meng Zhou,Rodrigo Aviles-Espinosa,Rupert Young,Philip Birch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用强化学习（RL）和多尺度反馈增强3D扩散模型的方法，通过PPO算法和结合2D切片评估与3D体积分析的新型奖励系统，优化医学图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型已成为3D医学图像生成的有力工具，但标准训练目标与临床相关性之间存在差距。需要开发能够同时优化局部纹理细节和全局结构一致性的方法，以提高生成图像的质量和临床实用性。

Method: 首先在MRI体积数据上预训练3D扩散模型建立生成先验，然后使用近端策略优化（PPO）进行微调，通过结合2D切片评估和3D体积分析的新型奖励系统提供多尺度反馈。

Result: 在BraTS 2019和OASIS-1数据集上的验证表明，RL反馈有效引导生成过程向更高质量分布发展。定量分析显示Fréchet Inception Distance显著改善，合成数据在肿瘤和疾病分类任务中表现出比未优化基线更强的实用性。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习与多尺度反馈结合的3D扩散模型优化方法，能够显著提升医学图像生成质量，增强合成数据在下游临床任务中的实用性，为医学图像生成提供了新的优化框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for 3D medical image generation, yet bridging the gap between standard training objectives and clinical relevance remains a challenge. This paper presents a method to enhance 3D diffusion models using Reinforcement Learning (RL) with multi-scale feedback. We first pretrain a 3D diffusion model on MRI volumes to establish a robust generative prior.
